## 🎯 Meta Philosophy & 4-Move Core Architecture

### ⚔️ Single Battle Architecture (Smogon & OP.GG)
In 1v1 Singles, battles are defined by tempo control, entry hazard pressure, and sweep setup:
* **Move 1: Primary STAB** — High-power, reliable damage matching the Pokémon's primary type.
* **Move 2: Coverage / Secondary STAB** — Targets defensive switch-ins that resist Move 1.
* **Move 3: Setup / Hazard Control** — Stat-boosting (*Swords Dance*, *Calm Mind*) or field traps (*Stealth Rock*, *Spikes*).
* **Move 4: Priority / Pivot / Recovery** — Revenge KO priority (*Aqua Jet*, *Extreme Speed*), momentum pivoting (*U-turn*, *Flip Turn*), or sustained longevity (*Roost*, *Recover*).

### 👥 Double Battle Architecture (Pikalytics & VGC)
In 2v2 Doubles, battles are defined by turn denial, board positioning, and burst spread damage:
* **Move 1: Protect / Shield** — Essential defensive maneuver to bait double-targets and preserve positioning.
* **Move 2: Spread Damage / Safe STAB** — Multi-target pressure (*Heat Wave*, *Hyper Voice*, *Rock Slide*) or single-target STAB that protects the ally from friendly fire.
* **Move 3: Speed Control / Priority Disruption** — Turn manipulation (*Fake Out*, *Grassy Glide*, *Tailwind*, *Icy Wind*).
* **Move 4: Pivoting / Coverage** — Rotational momentum (*Parting Shot*, *U-turn*) or tactical super-effective coverage.

## 🏆 S-Tier Meta Champions & Optimal Movesets

### 🌟 Garchomp
* **Rankings:** Single Battle: **#1** | Double Battle: **#2**
* **Base Stats Total (BST):** 600 (HP: 108, Atk: 130, Def: 95, SpA: 80, SpD: 85, Spe: 102)

#### ⚔️ Single Battle Meta Loadout (Smogon / 1v1 Hazard & Setup)
* **Role:** Fast Hazard Lead / Physical Wallbreaker
* **Optimal 4-Move Set:**
  1. **Earthquake** (Ground | Physical | Power: 100 | Acc: 100%) — *Hits all other Pokémon. Essential Ground STAB.*
  2. **Stealth Rock** (Rock | Status | Power: - | Acc: -%) — *Lays a trap of floating stones around opposing team on switch-in.*
  3. **Dragon Tail** (Dragon | Physical | Power: 60 | Acc: 90%) — *Forces enemy Pokémon out, dragging another into entry hazards.*
  4. **Draco Meteor** (Dragon | Special | Power: 130 | Acc: 90%) — *Devastating Dragon nuke; sharply drops user Sp. Atk.*
* **Key Alternate Moves:** Flamethrower, Outrage, Earth Power, Rock Tomb, Swords Dance, Stone Edge
* **Top 3 Items:** **Garchompite Z** (32.8%), **Choice Scarf** (18.8%), **Focus Sash** (16.7%)
* **Top Natures:** **Jolly** (+Spe/-SpA), **Adamant** (+Atk/-SpA)

#### 👥 Double Battle Meta Loadout (Pikalytics / VGC Synergy)
* **Role:** Special Sweeper / Mixed Mega Attacker
* **Optimal 4-Move Set:**
  1. **Protect** (Normal | Status | Power: - | Acc: -%) — *Enables user to protect itself from all attacks. Failing chance rises if used in succession.*
  2. **Power Gem** (Rock | Special | Power: 80 | Acc: 100%) — *Attacks with a ray of light that sparkles as if it were made of gemstones. Special Rock coverage.*
  3. **Earth Power** (Ground | Special | Power: 90 | Acc: 100%) — *Special Ground attack targeting physical walls. Safe from friendly fire and bypasses Intimidate.*
  4. **Draco Meteor** (Dragon | Special | Power: 130 | Acc: 90%) — *Comets are summoned down from the sky. Colossal Special Dragon burst; drops user Sp. Atk by 2.*
* **Key Alternate Moves:** Dragon Pulse, Rock Slide, Dragon Claw, Earthquake, Flamethrower, Stomping Tantrum
* **Top 3 Items:** **Garchompite Z** (51.8%), **Life Orb** (21.8%), **Choice Scarf** (9.6%)
* **Top Natures:** **Modest** (+SpA/-Atk), **Jolly** (+Spe/-SpA)
